# PIQA 全量 Bad Case 分析表

## 统计口径

- Bad case 并集：`acc=0` 或 `acc_norm=0` 任一成立。
- `acc` 答案：按 continuation 原始 log-likelihood 最大值选择。
- `acc_norm` 答案：严格按 LightEval 0.9.2 的字符长度归一化分数选择。
- 错误类型和可能原因是基于可复现规则的初筛，不能替代人工语义审阅。
- 总样本：1838；bad case 并集：787。

## 错误类型定义

| 错误类型 | 定义 |
|---|---|
| N1 归一化修正 | raw acc 选择错误，但字符长度归一化后的 acc_norm 选择正确。 |
| N2 归一化伤害 | raw acc 选择正确，但字符长度归一化后的 acc_norm 选择错误。 |
| P1 工具/材料可供性 | PIQA 中没有正确判断工具、材料或物体适合执行何种动作。 |
| P2 操作步骤/先后顺序 | PIQA 中混淆操作步骤、准备条件或动作先后顺序。 |
| P3 容器/流体/混合 | PIQA 中对盛装、倾倒、混合、密封或液体行为判断错误。 |
| P4 热力/烹饪/状态变化 | PIQA 中对加热、冷却、烹饪或材料状态变化判断错误。 |
| P5 清洁/去除/修复 | PIQA 中对清洁、去污、拆除、修补或维护方法判断错误。 |
| P6 安全/风险规避 | PIQA 中忽略危险、卫生、电气、火焰或防护约束。 |
| P7 日常物理常识 | PIQA 中更一般的物理常识、效果比较或题意歧义。 |

| 847 | N2 归一化伤害 | Question: To make Belgian waffles<br>Answer:<br><br>**候选：**<br>[0]  In a large bowl, stir together two tablespoons of self rising flour, 3/4 cup white sugar, and 3 3/4 teaspoons baking powder with a balloon whisk. Separate two eggs by cracking the shells on a flat surface, gently prying the broken shells apart, letting the whites slide through your fingers into one bowl while holding the unbroken yolks in your hand, to place in another bowl. Beat the yellow egg yolks together with a whisk, and add 1 1/2 cups milk, 1 teaspoon vanilla extract and 1 cup melted butter. Whisk. Add the wet mixture into the dry mixture and whisk again. Use a stand mixer with the whisk attachment fitted to whisk the whites until they hold firm peaks, standing straight up when you remove the whisk. Using a silicone spatula, gently fold the egg whites into the flour mixture by placing one spoonful of egg whites at a time into the bowl, lifting some of the mixture over the whites and repeating this gently until the whites are completely mixed in and the mixture is light and fluffy.
